15MB\n\ndef chunk_dir_path(chunk_index, depth):\n    \"\"\"Compute directory path for chunk_index with given depth.\"\"\"\n    digits = []\n    n = chunk_index\n    for _ in range(depth):\n        digits.append(n % 30)\n        n //= 30\n    digits.reverse()\n    return '/'.join(f'{d:02d}' for d in digits)\n\ndef main():\n    if len(sys.argv) != 3:\n        print(\"Usage: python compress.py <input_dir> <output_dir>\")\n        sys.exit(1)\n    \n    input_dir = Path(sys.argv[1]).resolve()\n    output_dir = Path(sys.argv[2]).resolve()\n    \n    if not input_dir.is_dir():\n        print(f\"Input directory not found: {input_dir}\")\n        sys.exit(1)\n    \n    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    \n    # Create tar\n    tar_path = output_dir / \"_temp.tar\"\n    with tarfile.open(tar_path, 'w') as tar:\n        tar.add(input_dir, arcname=input_dir.name)\n    \n    # Determine number of chunks\n    tar_size = tar_path.stat().st_size\n    num_chunks = math.ceil(tar_size / MAX_FILE_SIZE)\n    depth = max(1, math.ceil(math.log(num_chunks, 30))) if num_chunks > 1 else 1\n    \n    # Split tar into chunks\n    chunk_paths = []\n    with open(tar_path, 'rb') as f:\n        for i in range(num_chunks):\n            data = f.read(MAX_FILE_SIZE)\n            if not data:\n                break\n            dir_path = output_dir / chunk_dir_path(i, depth)\n            dir_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n            chunk_path = dir_path / f'chunk_{i:05d}.bin'\n            chunk_path.write_bytes(data)\n            chunk_paths.append(chunk_path)\n    \n    # Remove temp tar\n    tar_path.unlink()\n    \n    print(f\"Created {num_chunks} chunks in {output_dir}\")\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n```\n\nHmm wait, let me reconsider. Each top/XX has 30 entries (files). \n\nFor N=901, depth=3:\n- Chunk 0: digits=[0,0,0] -> \"00/00/00\"\n- Chunk 30: digits=[0,0,1] -> \"00/00/01\"\n- Chunk 899: digits=[29,29,0] -> \"00/29/29\"\n- Chunk 900: digits=[0,0,1] -> \"01/00/00\"\n\nWait, let me recompute. 900 in base 30 with 3 digits:\n- 900 = 30*30 + 0 = 30*30*1 + 30*0 + 0\n- digits: [1, 0, 0] (most significant first)\n- path: \"01/00/00\"\n\nOK so chunk 900 -> top/01/00/00.\n\n- top/00 has 30 entries (sub-dirs 00-29)\n- top/00/00 has 30 entries (chunks 0-29 as files)\n- top/00/01 has 30 entries (chunks 30-59 as files)\n- ...\n- top/00/29 has 30 entries (chunks 870-899 as files)\n- top/01 has 1 entry (sub-dir 00 with chunk 900)\n- top/02-top/29: 0 entries (don't create)\n\nTop has 2 entries (00, 01). Extract the resulting tar\n\nLet me also think about: what if a chunk file is missing or corrupt? Should I error out? Yes, definitely.\n\nLet me also consider performance. Tar'ing 783MB and then chunking should be fast. But for very large datasets, we might need streaming. Let me use streaming where possible:\n\nFor compress:\n- Stream tar creation (tarfile already does this)\n- Stream chunk creation (read 15MB at a time)\n\nFor decompress:\n- Stream chunk concatenation to tar (or to a temp file)\n- Stream tar extraction\n\nActually, tarfile already handles streaming. We can pipe the chunks directly to tarfile without a temp file.\n\nLet me code it:\n\n```python\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport tarfile\nimport math\nimport io\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nMAX_PER_DIR = 30\nMAX_FILE_SIZE = 15 * 1024 * 1024  # 15MB\nCHUNK_SIZE = MAX_FILE_SIZE\n\n\ndef chunk_dir_path(chunk_index, depth):\n    digits = []\n    n = chunk_index\n    for _ in range(depth):\n        digits.append(n % 30)\n        n //= 30\n    digits.reverse()\n    return '/'.join(f'{d:02d}' for d in digits)\n\n\ndef main():\n    if len(sys.argv) != 3:\n        print(\"Usage: python compress.py <input_dir> <output_dir>\", file=sys.stderr)\n        sys.exit(1)\n    \n    input_dir = Path(sys.argv[1]).resolve()\n    output_dir = Path(sys.argv[2]).resolve()\n    \n    if not input_dir.is_dir():\n        print(f\"Input directory not found: {input_dir}\", file=sys.stderr)\n        sys.exit(1)\n    \n    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    \n    # We need to know the tar size to determine chunk count.\n    # But tar is streaming, so we don't know size upfront.\n    # Solution: tar to a temp file first, then chunk it.\n    \n    import tempfile\n    with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(delete=False, suffix='.tar') as tmp:\n        tmp_path = Path(tmp.name)\n    \n    try:\n        with tarfile.open(tmp_path, 'w') as tar:\n            tar.add(input_dir, arcname='.')\n        \n        tar_size = tmp_path.stat().st_size\n        num_chunks = math.ceil(tar_size / CHUNK_SIZE)\n        depth = max(1, math.ceil(math.log(num_chunks, 30))) if num_chunks > 1 else 1\n        \n        with open(tmp_path, 'rb') as f:\n            for i in range(num_chunks):\n                data = f.read(CHUNK_SIZE)\n                if not data:\n                    break\n                dir_rel = chunk_dir_path(i, depth)\n                dir_path = output_dir / dir_rel\n                dir_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n                chunk_path = dir_path / f'chunk_{i:05d}.bin'\n                chunk_path.write_bytes(data)\n        \n        print(f\"Resharded: {tar_size} bytes -> {num_chunks} chunks in {output_dir}\")\n    finally:\n        tmp_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n```\n\nFor decompress:\n\n```python\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport tarfile\nimport re\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nCHUNK_PATTERN = re.compile(r'^chunk_(\\d{5})\\.bin$')\n\n\ndef main():\n    if len(sys.argv) != 2:\n        print(\"Usage: python decompress.py <resharded_dir>\", file=sys.stderr)\n        sys.exit(1)\n    \n    resharded_dir = Path(sys.argv[1]).resolve()\n    \n    if not resharded_dir.is_dir():\n        print(f\"Resharded directory not found: {resharded_dir}\", file=sys.stderr)\n        sys.exit(1)\n    \n    # Find all chunk files\n    chunks = []\n    for path in resharded_dir.rglob('chunk_*.bin'):\n        m = CHUNK_PATTERN.match(path.name)\n        if not m:\n            continue\n        idx = int(m.group(1))\n        chunks.append((idx, path))\n    \n    chunks.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])\n    \n    if not chunks:\n        print(\"No chunks found\", file=sys.stderr)\n        sys.exit(1)\n    \n    # Verify contiguous indices\n    for i, (idx, _) in enumerate(chunks):\n        if idx != i:\n            print(f\"Missing or out-of-order chunk at index {i}\", file=sys.stderr)\n            sys.exit(1)\n    \n    # Concatenate chunks and extract\n    import tempfile\n    \n    # Extract to a temp dir first to avoid conflicts with chunk dirs\n    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp_dir:\n        tmp_path = Path(tmp_dir)\n        \n        # Stream chunks into tarfile\n        with tarfile.open(fileobj=open(tmp_path / 'combined.tar', 'wb'), mode='w|') as tar:\n            for idx, chunk_path in chunks:\n                with open(chunk_path, 'rb') as f:\n                    while True:\n                        data = f.read(15 * 1024 * 1024)\n                        if not data:\n                            break\n                        tar.fileobj.write(data)\n                #
